It is described how they are authored against the graph and how they serve several purposes: guiding users through an unfamiliar graph, creating reproducible narratives, and surfacing data-quality issues previously hidden in aggregate statistics. The authoring platform LODEON including its Sparnatural and AI-supported authoring assistants is introduced as a proof-of-concept. Within the authoring environment, every claim made about the data can be backed by an explicit query, making these narratives transparent and reproducible. This paper also reflects on lessons learned from hands-on seminars and workshops. Early experience suggests that such data stories make cultural-heritage knowledge graphs more accessible for both exploration and quality assessment.

</details>


### [185] [LLMs as Post-hoc Auditors of Physiological Plausibility in Symbolic Regression: A Clinician-Evaluated Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11431)
*Jorge López-Varela,J. Ignacio Hidalgo,José-Manuel Muñoz,Omar Costilla-Reyes,Esther Maqueda,Jesus Moreno-Fernandez,Tomás González-Vidal,J. Manuel Velasco,Oscar Garnica*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大语言模型在提高由进化计算方法生成的符号回归模型可解释性方面的潜力，尽管模型仍存在一些质疑的解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 符号回归模型虽然在预测准确性方面出色，但复杂的结构和科学不一致依然存在。研究旨在通过大语言模型的辅助，提高这些模型的可解释性和医疗合理性。

Method: 研究基于作者之前关于使用基于语法的遗传编程估计体型脂肪百分比的工作，通过让大语言模型对四个符号表达式进行分析和排名来评估模型。

Result: 三个大语言模型在排名输出方面得到了临床医生的更积极评价，但在解释方面仍提出了生理学和数学上的疑问，表明大语言模型更适合在专家监督下进行比对审核，而不是独立验证。

Conclusion: 大语言模型在提高符号回归模型的可解释性和医疗合理性方面具有潜力，但仍然存在一定的局限性，需要专家的监督来确保模型的合理解释。

Abstract: Genetic Programming and its variants, such as grammatical evolution, are widely used in Symbolic Regression to derive mathematical expressions from multivariate data. In addition to predictive accuracy, models are appreciated for their potential to provide interpretability, offering explicit equations that relate input variables to outcomes. However, achieving interpretability and plausibility remains challenging, as evolved models may be complex or scientifically inconsistent. In this study, we explore whether Large Language Models, can assist in improving the explainability of Symbolic Regression models generated by evolutionary computation methods. Building upon our previous work on estimating body fat percentage using grammar-based Genetic Programming , we investigate the use of LLMs as post-processing tools to analyze and rank evolved expressions according to their interpretability and medical plausibility. Four symbolic expressions are analysed by three LLMs over three repeated runs, and the resulting interpretations and rankings are assessed by a panel of three clinicians. Across the three LLMs, comparative model-ranking outputs received more favorable clinician assessments than isolated term-level interpretations. However, the LLMs also produced physiologically and mathematically questionable explanations, indicating that they are better suited to comparative auditing under expert oversight than to autonomous validation.\blfootnote{The present work is an extended version of a paper submitted into a journal.

</details>


### [186] [Calibration-Aware Uncertainty Cascades for Efficient Heterogeneous Model Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11446)
*Yilin Zhang,Han Jiang,Cai Xu,Ying Liu,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAUC是一种后处理框架，它独立校准每个模型的信心，并利用验证数据选择部署策略。这使得部署策略与特定模型池或预算无关，从而在不同基准测试中提高了预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的模型协作方法通常依赖于固定的模型池和操作预算，难以适应变化。

Method: 提出了一种名为Calibration-Aware Uncertainty Cascades (CAUC)的简单后处理框架，通过对每个模型进行独立校准，并使用验证数据选择部署策略。

Result: CAUC在六项语言基准测试中平均相对准确性提高了1.9%，同时减少了约47%的强模型调用，在图像分类基准测试中保持或提高了预测性能，减少测量的GFLOPs高达57%。

Conclusion: 通过实验验证了CAUC方法的有效性，并证明了校准信任度阈值具有显式的选择性风险解释，而未校准的得分则没有类似的可靠性保证。

Abstract: Heterogeneous model collaboration seeks to exploit the complementary strengths of different models to balance predictive performance and inference cost. Existing approaches typically rely either on trained routers, which tie routing decisions to a fixed task and model pool, or on raw-confidence cascades, whose thresholds lack consistent reliability semantics across heterogeneous models. Consequently, these approaches adapt poorly to changing model pools and deployment budgets. We propose Calibration-Aware Uncertainty Cascades (CAUC), a simple post-hoc framework that independently calibrates each model's confidence and selects deployment policies using validation data. The resulting calibrated confidence scores establish a common reliability scale for accepting an early prediction, invoking a stronger model, or selectively combining model outputs. This unified decision criterion decouples deployment policies from any particular model pool or operating budget. We further show theoretically that calibration gives confidence thresholds an explicit selective-risk interpretation, whereas uncalibrated scores offer no comparable reliability guarantee. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, across six language benchmarks, CAUC achieves an average relative accuracy improvement of 1.9% over strong-model-only inference while avoiding approximately 47% of strong-model calls. On image classification benchmarks, it maintains or improves predictive performance while reducing measured GFLOPs by up to 57%.

</details>


### [187] [RouteRepair: Instance-Level Failure Diagnosis and Targeted Repair in LLM-Based Automated Heuristic Design for Routing Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11452)
*Binghao Ji,Di Huang,Jiahui Fang,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RouteRepair通过诊断具体实例结构的弱点并进行针对性修改，改善了抽样评估中未显现的特定失败案例，显著提升了旅行商问题（TSP）和装箱车辆路径问题（CVRP）的路由规则性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来，基于大型语言模型（LLM）的自动化启发式设计方法在生成有效的路由规则方面显示出潜在优势。然而，这些方法可能会因实例结构不同而出现重叠的失败案例，使得最终评估可能无法准确反映每个启发式规则的实际效果。

Method: RouteRepair采用了一种结合了路由证据、求解器行为和程序上下文的策略，来定义修补目标，并通过匹配父级-子级评估来验证每个干预措施的有效性。该方法主要应用在构造搜索，引导式局部搜索和蚂蚁优化算法上。

Result: 实验结果显示，使用RouteRepair方法改进后的引导式局部搜索（GLS）有效降低了旅行商问题（TSP）的最佳性差距，从1.7476%降至0.7587%；同时，在含装箱约束的车辆路径问题（CVRP）中，基于改进的启发式规则也降低了平均路径成本。产生的基于蚂蚁优化（ACO）的先验信息也优于对手设计的先验信息。

Conclusion: RouteRepair在维护良好性能的同时，能够有效地改进在困难案例中的路由启发式规则，展示了基于失败意识的、证据约束完善方法的有效性。

Abstract: Efficient routing optimization is essential to freight transportation, urban logistics, and shared mobility, where high-quality heuristics are often required under limited computational budgets. Recent large language model (LLM)-based automated heuristic design methods can generate effective routing rules, but aggregate evaluation may mask recurrent failures on particular instance structures. To address this limitation, this study develops RouteRepair, which diagnoses parent-specific weaknesses from instance-level performance and applies targeted modifications to the corresponding heuristic components while protecting behavior that already performs well. Routing evidence, solver behavior, and program context are combined to define bounded repair objectives, and each intervention is validated through matched parent-child evaluation of failure recovery and collateral degradation. Experiments on the traveling salesman problem (TSP) and capacitated vehicle routing problem (CVRP) span constructive search, guided local search, and ant colony optimization. RouteRepair-GLS reduces the mean TSP optimality gap from 1.7476% to 0.7587%, while the constructive CVRP heuristic lowers average route cost by 1.91% relative to the savings heuristic; the generated ACO priors also outperform matched hand-designed priors. These results show that failure-aware, evidence-constrained refinement can improve routing heuristics on difficult instances while preserving performance on cases they already solve well.

</details>


### [188] [Flexible and Interpretable Accent Distance Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11458)
*Charles McGhee,Mark J. F. Gales,Kate M. Knill*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种通过发音倒置生成可解释的发音表示方法，并利用最佳运输框架进行任意录音类型的音调比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文的目标是填补现有发音比较方法的空白，提供一种既可以对准发音差异，又可以避免现有方法时间耗费和不可解释性的方法。

Method: 本文通过使用发音倒置生成发音表示，并应用最佳运输理论进行发音比较。

Result: 研究表明，该方法能够有效比较不同发音人的发音差异，并且生成的发音表示具有较高的可解释性。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种新的发音比较方法，该方法克服了现有方法的缺陷，并为语音技术和语言学研究提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Determining the differences between two speakers' accents is a fundamental task in linguistics and speech technology research. The methodology used to measure these differences depends on the specific research area. A phonetics researcher may demonstrate accent variation by comparing vowel formants in paired recordings of individual words. These results will be interpretable, but the recordings will be time-consuming to collect and may not be representative of connected speech. Accented Text-to-Speech (TTS) research has pushed towards using accent embeddings derived from accent classification tasks. These embeddings can be produced from any speech recording, but are not readily interpretable. In this paper, we demonstrate that articulatory representations created through articulatory inversion can be used as an interpretable basis for accent comparison and that optimal transport provides a framework for accent comparison across arbitrary recording types.

</details>


### [189] [The Convention Gap: Towards Measuring Implicit Communication in Cooperative AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11489)
*Makoto Fukushima,Hua-Dong Xiong,Ehsan Moradi Pari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究通过重新播放大量汉诺塔游戏行动，发现人类团队的表现明显优于AI团队和人机组合团队，尤其在未获得提示的情况下，人类团队的优势更为显著。研究还提出了‘公约差距’的概念，用于衡量AI与人类之间隐含沟通的能力差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的AI-AI评估基准可能无法捕捉人类合作中的隐含规则，即人类基于共享协议理解超文本信息的能力，因此提出了‘公约差距’的概念。

Method: 研究通过重新播放三组公开数据集的汉诺塔游戏行动，计算不同团队在接收到不同类型信息后的失败概率，以及‘公约差距’的数值。

Result: 研究表明，人类团队的公约差距为+26.2%，而AI团队为-0.7%，人机组合团队为+16.4%。在人类团队中，未收到任何提示时的公约差距为+46%。在人机组合中，尽管不同AI伙伴提供的人类可以利用的直接信息相似，但人类失败率差异显著，且与AI相对应的公约差距也不同。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，人类与AI之间的沟通不一致可能是决定AI与人类合作效果的关键因素。

Abstract: Cooperative AI agents are evaluated against other AIs, yet human cooperation relies on implicit conventions---shared protocols for reading meaning beyond the literal message---which AI-AI benchmarks may not capture. We propose the \emph{convention gap}, the difference between the failure probability predicted from the literal content of communication and the observed failure rate, as a metric of implicit communication. In the card game Hanabi, the finite deck and deterministic hint constraints make this posterior exactly computable. We replayed about 101,000 play actions from three public datasets of human-human (hanab.live), AI-AI (HOAD), and human-AI (HanabiData) games. The gap was +26.2 percentage points (pp) in human pairs, $-$0.7~pp in AI pairs, and +16.4~pp in human-AI pairs, and was concentrated on plays of cards that had received no hints (+46~pp in human pairs). Within human-AI play, the literal information available to humans was similar across the three AI partners (mean predicted failure 38--41\%), but human failure rates ranged from 14.4\% to 34.4\% and the gap from +24.1 to +6.2~pp; the partner eliciting the largest gap produced the fewest human failures. Game score carried different information: it depended on each corpus's roster composition, whereas the gap separated human from AI play at the agent level. As a known-answer check, Off-Belief Learning agents, whose convention content is controlled by construction, gave a gap of +1.6~pp at the convention-free level, rising monotonically to +21.7~pp. These results suggest that convention compatibility, rather than AI-AI performance, may predict an AI's effectiveness with human partners.

</details>


### [190] [Published Unlearning Numbers Move Per Checkpoint, and Not Because the Removed Data Survives: An Audit of 263 Released Batch-Normalized Checkpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11490)
*Junlong Shen Xingyu Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种“卸载审计”，通过比较未学习模型和重新训练的参考模型发布的数据，以及不参与梯度更新和不包含发布记录的批标准化统计量，来评估模型的重新训练情况。研究发现，即使平均值未发生变化，某些检查点的状态仍会偏离，而更换保留数据对发布的决策影响有限，但检查点的演化状态可以反映其变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在评估在发布模型后重新训练模型对其决策结果的影响，提出了一种新的审计方法‘卸载审计’。

Method: 研究方法包括使用两个模型进行比较：一个是未学习的模型，另一个是重新训练后的参考模型。通过分析它们发布的计算结果和批标准化统计量来评估重新训练情况。分析未参与梯度更新的模型状态以及更换数据集对模型决策的影响。

Result: 研究发现，即使平均值没有变化，模型的状态也可能发生变化。更换保留的训练数据对发布的决策影响有限，而检查点的状态变化可以反映模型的演化情况。评估结果显示，改变检查点状态对决策结果的影响较小。

Conclusion: 研究结论是，发布模型时应详细说明其训练协议和批标准化统计量，以便更好地评估模型的重新训练对其决策结果的具体影响。

Abstract: An unlearning audit reads its verdict off numbers that an unlearned model and its retrained reference each publish, and both also ship batch-normalization statistics that no gradient step wrote and no release records. Refitting them on kept data at bit-identical weights moves 47 of 221 released checkpoints past the spread their own release's seeds show, several inside a method whose average does not move: what moves is the checkpoint's property, not its method's. What does the moving is not the removed data surviving in the state: exchanging kept records for removed ones inside a fixed fitting pool moves a published cell by almost nothing, while how far a checkpoint's shipped state has drifted from any refit does track it. The consequence for a published decision is real but narrow: twelve verdicts cross, four clear a measured recalibration budget, two clear it on every replicate, and a population we trained and sited near its own criterion yields none. A release should therefore name the fitting convention beside the number, on the batch-normalized vision models where this channel exists.

</details>


### [191] [From Document Silos to Process Intelligence: A Multi-Layer Knowledge Graph for CMC Process Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11493)
*Reza Amirmoshiri,Faryad Sahneh,Yasser Jangjou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个模块化的代理型AI平台，将不均匀的工艺开发文档转换为可查询的双层知识图谱，从而解决科学转移和监管申报中的可追溯性和高昂知识管理成本问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在药物发现到商业化生产的多阶段、知识密集型连续过程中，CMC过程开发产生的大量技术信息被分散在不同的功能和异构格式中，这导致了科学转让和监管申报中的可追溯性缺口和显著的知识管理成本。

Method: 该平台采用一个基础层的知识图谱和一个智能层的知识图谱。基础层构建一个无损的文档-节段-片段层次结构的词法图，通过数字、扫描、手写和多语言文档。智能层提取对齐的本体实体，并通过源出处锚定领域图桥接跨文档概念。两层均由LLM代理操作，选择最适合每个问题的检索路径。

Result: 在505个问题中，95%的多选题准确率显示了平台的强大可靠性。但85%的严格LLM裁判通过率在比较和整个语料库问题上有所下降，揭示了Tier-1准确性无法捕捉的失败分类。路由代理根据问题类型在两个层次之间进行选择。

Conclusion: 本研究表明了图基架构在未来将应用于更多的药企来实现碎片化文档库向结构化过程智能的转换，并期待未来平台设计师能够使用该协议评估他们的系统以针对非公共数据库，从而促进机器人平台在药企中的采用。

Abstract: Chemistry, Manufacturing and Controls (CMC) process development generates an enormous body of technical information across a multi-stage, knowledge-intensive continuum from drug discovery to commercial manufacturing. This knowledge is traditionally fragmented across functions and heterogeneous formats, causing traceability gaps and significant knowledge-management costs during technology transfer and regulatory filing. We present a modular agentic-AI platform that converts a heterogeneous corpus of process-development documents into a queryable, dual-layer knowledge graph. A base knowledge layer builds a lexical graph with a Document-Section-Chunk hierarchy through lossless ingestion of digital, scanned, handwritten, and multilingual documents, while an intelligence layer extracts ontology-aligned entities and bridges cross-document concepts through a provenance-anchored domain graph. LLM agents operate across both layers, selecting the retrieval path best suited to each question. We evaluate the lexical layer with a novel three-tier protocol measuring the deployment-fidelity of a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system on proprietary data, demonstrated on 505 questions curated from 38 development reports of a Sanofi small-molecule program. Tier-1 multiple-choice accuracy of 95% signals strong platform reliability; the stricter Tier-2 LLM-judge pass rate of 85%, which degrades on comparative and corpus-wide questions, reveals a failure taxonomy that Tier-1 accuracy alone fails to capture. A router agent selects between layers according to question type. We anticipate this protocol will enable future designers of agentic platforms to assess their systems against nonpublic databases, and that graph-based architectures will see broader adoption in pharma as a means of transforming fragmented document repositories into structured process intelligence.

</details>


### [192] [ActMap: Single-Pass Uncertainty Quantification from Generation-Time Activation Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11498)
*Jacopo Dardini,Roberta Calegari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ActMap 是一种白盒表示，通过简化生成时的隐藏状态轨迹，以固定形状的张量压缩生成过程中的所有细节，从而能够在单次生成中量化不确定性。使用小型 Vision Transformer 分类器或同等容量的多层感知机可以从 ActMap 中直接读取正确性概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的不确定性量化方法存在一些不足，例如需要多次采样、仅读取输出标记的概率或简化模型内部计算。ActMap 提供了一种新的解决方案，通过保留隐藏状态轨迹的时间结构，并使用小型视图变压器分类器或同等容量的多层感知机来直接读取正确性概率。

Method: ActMap 通过在生成过程中实时生成一个固定形状的张量（时空统计通道），将生成过程中的所有层和生成的每一个标记压缩为一个固定的 12×32×128 尺寸的张量。然后通过一个轻量级的分类器，如视图变压器或同等容量的多层感知机，从该张量中确定正确性概率。

Result: 在几个指令调优的 7-8B 大型语言模型上，在短答案问答、直接答案数学和总结可信度任务中，ActMap 优于现有的不确定性量化方法，如采样、标记概率、注意力和嵌入表示。并且 ActMap 在十个十二个配对中表现出较低的校准误差，达到了与 ACT-ViT 类似的效果。

Conclusion: ActMap 通过提供一种简洁且高效的方法来量化大型语言模型的不确定性，为全面监控部署模型的不同程序提供了实用的基础。

Abstract: Practical uncertainty quantification (UQ) for large language models must decide,
  from a single generation, whether a specific answer should be trusted. Existing
  methods either sample multiple generations, read only output-token probabilities,
  or reduce the model's internal computation to a single hidden state. We introduce
  ActMap, a white-box representation that compresses the generation-time hidden-
  state trajectory (every layer, every generated token) into a fixed $12 \times 32
  \times 128$ tensor of temporal-statistic channels that preserves structure across
  transformer depth and pooled hidden coordinates. The map is captured during the
  generation pass with no measurable overhead, has a fixed shape across model
  depths and hidden sizes, and occupies 96 KiB: a compact artifact that can be
  retained for audit-relevant generations and probed directly, with occlusion
  analysis localizing the classifier's signal to mid-depth regions of the map. A
  lightweight classifier, instantiated as a compact Vision Transformer, reads an
  estimated correctness probability from each map in a fraction of a millisecond;
  capacity-matched MLPs perform comparably, indicating the representation itself
  carries the result. Trained and evaluated in-domain on short-answer QA, direct-
  answer math, and summarization factuality with three instruction-tuned 7-8B
  models, ActMap consistently outperforms sampling, token-probability, attention,
  and embedding baselines, and matches ACT-ViT, a detector trained on dense
  activation tensors $67 \times$ larger, at essentially the same mean AUROC with
  lower calibration error on ten of twelve pairs. The resulting score supports
  abstention, routing, and selective verification from a single generation, making
  it a practical primitive for scalable oversight of deployed models.

</details>


### [193] [Extending SMT Solving with Non-Ground Clause Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11509)
*Yasmine Briefs,Christoph Weidenbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的SMT求解器策略，结合了基于实例的推理论证和非实例化冲突分析，使其能够在不牺牲问题规模的情况下，以更简洁的证明找到冲突


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的非基于实例的SMT求解器在遇到从非实例化公理产生的冲突时，仅能学到实例化了的冲突，尽管非实例化推理可以提供比纯实例化推理更短的证明显性。因此，为了提高SMT求解的效率并获得更简洁的证明，提出了结合基于实例化推理和非实例化冲突分析的新方法

Method: 提出了一种新的求解器框架，该框架包括基于实例化的推理论证，CDCL(T)-风格的推理规则，以及非实例化的冲突分析。求解过程中，使用基于实例化的推理来处理地面实例，而在冲突分析中则在原始的非地面公理上进行

Result: 新方法可以在保持问题规模的情况下提供更短的证明，且通过合适的策略，所获的冲突学习结果可能是非冗余的。此外，该方法还展示了如何将时间回溯纳入SMT求解，证明了其能够模拟CDCL、SCL(FOL)、SCL(T)甚至Res解决问题的方法

Conclusion: 提出的新求解框架成功地将非实例化冲突学习纳入了实例化的SMT求解器中，从而提升了SMT求解的效率和证明的简洁性。

Abstract: Quantifier instantiation is currently the main approach to non-ground SMT solving: solvers generate ground instances and solve the resulting ground SMT problems with CDCL(T)-style reasoning. When a conflict is found, conflict analysis learns only a ground clause, even though the conflict comes from instances of non-ground clauses. Yet non-ground reasoning can give exponentially shorter proofs than purely ground reasoning. We propose a calculus that consists of ground instantiations, CDCL(T)-style rules, and non-ground conflict analysis. The solver reasons on ground instances, but the resolution steps of conflict analysis are performed on their original non-ground clauses. This produces learned clauses that are typically more general than the ground conflict. With a suitable strategy, the learned clauses are even non-redundant. We also show how chronological backtracking can be included in SMT solving. Our calculus gives a common setting for CDCL(T)-style SMT solving, a range of instantiation-based procedures, and non-ground clause learning, and we prove that it simulates CDCL, SCL(FOL), SCL(T), and even Resolution.

</details>


### [194] [Lightweight LiDAR-Based Cone Detection Framework Using Random Forest for Formula Student Driverless](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11527)
*Márk Mező-Kerekes,Péter Praksz,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的仅基于LiDAR的感知管道，专为CPU执行设计。该管道通过地面去除、IMU基于的运动补偿、DBSCAN聚类和基于几何特征的随机森林分类，实现了在CPU上的低延迟高性能感知，经实测达到98.33%的F1分数和3.13毫秒的端到端运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动驾驶汽车感知管道大多依赖深度学习和多传感器融合，通常需要GPU加速。而本文旨在提供一种仅使用LiDAR、专为CPU设计的轻量级感知管道，以满足低成本和低延迟要求，特别适用于资源受限的自动驾驶方程式赛车。

Method: 该感知管道由四部分组成：地面去除、基于IMU的运动补偿、DBSCAN聚类和基于几何特征的随机森林分类。通过特征重要性分析，模型输入从12个特征减少到7个，但仍保持了卓越的性能。

Result: 在实际FSD事件中收集的2,371个标记簇上进行了评估，该管道实现了98.33%的F1分数和3.13毫秒的端到端运行时间。此外，还提供了数据集、标注工具和训练模型，以便其他团队参考。

Conclusion: 本文提出的方法为资源受限的自动驾驶方程式赛车团队提供了一个实用且可复制的基准线，可以支持他们在低成本硬件上实现可靠且低延迟的感知。

Abstract: Reliable, low-latency perception is crucial for Formula Student Driverless vehicles, yet many existing pipelines rely on deep learning and multi-sensor fusion, often requiring GPU acceleration. This paper presents a lightweight LiDAR-only perception pipeline tailored for CPU execution, combining ground removal, IMU-based motion compensation, DBSCAN clustering, and geometric feature-based Random Forest classification. Feature importance analysis reduced the model input from 12 to 7 features while preserving performance. Evaluated on 2,371 labeled clusters collected from real FSD events, the pipeline achieves an F1-score of 98.33% and an end-to-end runtime of 3.13 ms on CPU-only hardware. The released dataset, labeling tool, and trained models provide a practical and reproducible baseline for other resource-constrained autonomous racing teams.

</details>


### [195] [Prompt Revision as a Source of Cultural Bias in Text-to-Image Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11532)
*Aleksandra Urman,Elsa Lichtenegger,Salima Jaoua,Azza Bouleimen,Robin Forsberg,Corinna Hertweck,Stefania Ionescu,Nicolò Pagan,Ancsa Hannak,Joachim Baumann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出WORLDVIEW基准，包含8960个跨15种语言和31种语言-上下文配对的提示，对DALL-E-3、Imagen-4和GPT-Image-1.5三个系统的修订层进行了审计。结果发现，非西方和非盎格鲁-撒克逊文化的上下文被过度标记，词汇单一化并呈现出刻板印象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文化偏见审计仅关注最终生成的图像，未能深入分析导致偏见的具体过程和环节，尤其是忽视了在图像生成过程中透明度较低的修订层问题。研究者希望通过审计修订层，揭示并解决在图像生成过程中可能存在的文化偏见。

Method: 研究者创建了WORLDVIEW基准，包含8960个提示，覆盖15种语言和31种语言-上下文配对。通过三步分析（标记程度、单一同质化、刻板印象）评估各系统的修订层，比较原始提示和未经修订处理的提示生成的图像，确定修订层是导致文化刻板印象的潜在原因。

Result: 研究发现，美国的上下文最轻度标记，而非西方和非盎格鲁-撒克逊文化的上下文被明显标记得更重，词汇被简化为狭隘的词汇集，适用于不同主题的提示，并简化为可识别的文化刻板印象。

Conclusion: 研究指出，对于识别和修正文化偏见，需要审计实际部署的系统，而不仅仅是模型本身；修订层是导致文化刻板印象的一个隐藏且未被记录的原因。

Abstract: Commercial text-to-image systems silently revise user prompts before generating images, a step users typically cannot disable or even see. Yet, existing audits of cultural bias examine only the final images and treat generation as a single pipeline, so they cannot tell where the bias originates. We introduce WORLDVIEW, a multilingual benchmark of 8,960 prompts across 15 languages and 31 language-context pairings. Using it, we audit the revision layer in three systems (DALL-E-3, Imagen-4, GPT-Image-1.5) through a three-step analysis of how heavily it marks each cultural context, whether it flattens that context into a narrow vocabulary, and whether that vocabulary is stereotypical. Relative to a no-context English baseline, the US is the least-marked context, while non-Western and non-Anglophone contexts are marked far more heavily, flattened into narrow vocabularies applied across topically diverse prompts, and reduced to recognizable cultural stereotypes. Comparing images from original versus revised prompts on models without a revision layer, we identify the layer itself as a previously undocumented, causal source of this stereotyping. To locate cultural bias, and fix it, we must audit the system as deployed, not the model alone.

</details>


### [196] [Characterizing Job Power Elasticity for Power-Flexible AI Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11542)
*Philip Colangelo,Charles Dawson,Shayan Sengupta,Ayse Coskun,Varun Sivaram*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统地对大规模语言模型训练的工作负载弹性进行了表征，引入了功率灵活性指数 (PFI) 来量化功率减少的成本，并展示了 PFI 意识下的功率分配在功率限制下可以最大化总吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型训练是现代数据中心增长最快的电力消耗来源，而电力可用性是阻碍人工智能基础设施进一步发展的主要瓶颈。为了利用功率弹性来促进人工智能增长、限制电力成本，并提高现有电网基础设施的利用率，首先需要了解降低 GPU 功率对训练工作负载性能的影响。

Method: 论文通过对 131 次大规模语言模型训练运行的数据收集和分析，计算了不同配置和任务类型下的功率灵活性指数 (PFI)，并通过实际功率分配实验来验证其在功率受限环境下的性能。

Result: 研究发现大规模语言模型训练工作的功率弹性显著但变化较大；并识别到了运行时可以预测 PFI 的遥测信号。PFI 意识下的功率分配能在 30% 功率减少的情况下恢复约 1,500 值 / 秒的吞吐量。

Conclusion: 本文的研究结果已经确定了工作负载功率弹性作为可量化属性，为其在人工智能基础设施中的应用奠定了基础，同时提供了电网响应型、功率感知的 AI 基础设施的基石。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) training is among the fastest-growing sources of electricity demand in modern data centers, and power availability is a primary bottleneck to continued AI infrastructure growth. Making the power consumption of these workloads flexible could unlock additional power for AI growth, limit increases in electricity prices, and improve the utilization of existing grid infrastructure. However, to realize this flexibility, we must first understand how the performance of training workloads changes when GPU power is reduced.
  This paper presents the first systematic characterization of \emph{job power elasticity} (the sensitivity of throughput to power reductions) in LLM training. To quantify elasticity, we introduce the \emph{Power Flexibility Index (PFI)}, a normalized metric that quantifies the performance cost of power reductions and provides a control primitive for SLA-aware power flexibility.
  We collect data from 131 LLM training runs on H200 (plus 24 H200 validation runs and 34 matched H100 runs), including both dense and mixture-of-experts models, pretraining and fine-tuning tasks, and up to 32 GPUs. We find that LLM training jobs exhibit substantial but variable power elasticity, and we identify telemetry signals that predict PFI at runtime. Finally, we demonstrate that PFI-aware power allocation maximizes total tokens/second throughput under power constraints. Under a 30\% power reduction, PFI-aware power allocation recovers ~1.5k tokens/s per job, 63\% of the performance gap between an equal-weight allocation and an oracle with perfect information. Our results establish power elasticity as a measurable property of training jobs and provide a foundation for power-aware, grid-responsive AI infrastructure.

</details>


### [197] [Enabling Knowledge Graph Understanding at Scale with the EXplore Your Graphs ENgine (EXYGEN)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11569)
*Harshdeep Singh,Yurui Zhu,Giovanni Colavizza,Matteo Romanello*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出EXYGEN框架，通过文本到SPARQL生成任务探索了KG理解的对话访问能力，使用非细调的方法实现了良好性能，并介绍了一种高效并保持结构多样性的图样本策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索如何利用大规模知识图谱（KG），通过小样本和自动衍生的结构化元数据，结合大型语言模型（LLM）生成技术，实现无需特定任务调优的对话访问。

Method: 该方法首先利用文本到SPARQL生成任务，结合检索增强生成（RAG）管道，使用VoID描述和ShEx schema，并进行模型消融研究；其次，通过一种预测覆盖率意识的并行图样本策略，在大规模KG中生成必要的结构化元数据。

Result: 实验结果显示，结合ShEx schema、检索三元组以及示例问题-查询对，实现了0.419的执行结果精确匹配率，且无需任何LLM的具体调优。还发现，对于大规模KG的元数据生成，传统的基于覆盖率的方法会导致计算不可行，引入的策略能有效保持结构多样性，减少运算时间超过80倍。

Conclusion: 研究结论表明，基于结构化模式的上下文和轻量级提示可以显著减少对调优的依赖，实现大规模KG的对话访问，但仍需进一步减少对人工标注示例的依赖，以完全实现模型的灵活性和泛化能力。

Abstract: We present EXYGEN (EXplore Your Graphs ENgine), a framework for knowledge graph (KG) understanding that enables conversational access to KGs at scale. We address two questions in sequence. First, how effectively can LLMs perform text-to-SPARQL generation given only automatically derived structured metadata and small graph samples, rather than task-specific fine-tuning? We integrate VoID descriptions and ShEx schemas into a retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline and ablate KG-derived context on the SciQA benchmark. Our best configuration -- combining ShEx schemas, retrieved triples, and example question-query pairs -- reaches an exact match of 0.419 on execution results without any LLM fine-tuning. We further find that lexical metrics such as F1 poorly predict query correctness, and that larger general-purpose LLMs can outperform smaller code-specialized ones once given sufficient context. Second, we ask how to generate the structured metadata that this method relies on from very large KGs, where KG metadata generation becomes computationally intractable. We introduce a predicate-coverage-aware parallel graph sampling strategy that preserves structural diversity while remaining computationally tractable. On OpenCitations Meta and GESIS, it retains high predicate coverage with minimal triple loss and reduces runtime by over 80x; on ORKG, sampling is not just faster but the only tractable path to obtain complete metadata. Together, these results show that structured schema context and lightweight prompting can substantially reduce reliance on fine-tuning for scalable conversational access to KGs, though closing the remaining gap to fully fine-tuned approaches will likely require reducing dependence on curated question-query exemplars -- whether through synthetic generation or an execution-feedback-driven approach -- and validating these findings beyond a single benchmark.

</details>


### [198] [Making Alternative Data Work: Context-Augmented LLMs for Financial Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11607)
*Jihoon Kwon,Lawrence Liu,Daekyung Park,Sumin Kim,Haverty Jack,Hoyoung Lee,Katherine Bjorkman,Josh McKenney,Peter Laurelli,Nicole Kagan,Zach Golkhou,Thorsten Neumann,Edward Tong,Pete Petersen,Yoon Kim,Alejandro Lopez-Lira,Yongjae Lee,Chanyeol Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究探索了利用大型语言模型（LLMs）结合非传统数据源预测公司财务表现的可能性，提出了一种双代理框架进行上下文学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的财务数据可能不足以充分反映公司的运营活动和市场状况，而从消费交易、网络流量和预测市场等非传统数据源中收集的数据能够在及时性和信息全面性方面提供补充，但这些数据难以灵活地整合到传统的预测模型中。因此，本研究旨在探讨LLMs是否能通过整合非传统数据和其他财务信息来预测公司表现。

Method: 研究提出了一个双代理框架，首先确定每个非传统数据渠道对哪些公司的信息是有用的，然后使用公司和渠道特定的上下文来预测收入。

Result: 实验结果显示，结合非传统数据和财务信息可以提高LLM的预测准确性，这些预测比标准预测基线更加准确。

Conclusion: 研究结论认为，LLMs提供了一种灵活且实用的方法，可用于整合异构财务信息和非传统数据进行公司绩效预测。

Abstract: When forecasting a firm's future financial performance, alternative data - data collected from non-traditional sources such as consumer transactions, web traffic, and prediction markets - can provide timely signals about firms' operating activities and broader market conditions. These signals may reveal information that is not captured by traditional public sources and can therefore provide complementary information for forecasting firms' future financial performance. However, firm-level alternative data often have limited historical coverage, are relevant only to specific prediction targets or subsets of firms, and are distributed across numerous heterogeneous channels, making them difficult to incorporate flexibly into conventional forecasting approaches. Meanwhile, large language models (LLMs) can interpret instructions, learn from in-context examples, and generate predictions by combining heterogeneous information without task-specific parameter updates. Motivated by this potential flexibility, we investigate whether an LLM can forecast firm performance by integrating alternative data with other financial information through in-context learning. We propose a two-agent framework that first identifies the firms for which each alternative data channel is likely to be informative and then predicts revenue using firm- and channel-specific context. We evaluate the framework across four commercial alternative data channels. In our experiments, adding alternative data in context alongside other financial information improves the LLM's forecasting relative to either source alone, and these forecasts are more accurate than those of standard forecasting baselines. These findings suggest that LLMs provide a flexible and practical approach to integrating alternative data with heterogeneous financial information.

</details>


### [199] [SIRF: A Spec-Internalized Risk Foundation Model for Industrial Content Risk Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11752)
*Suwan Wu,Yumeng Lin,Pengcheng Yuan,Xiaolong Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SIRF 是一种平台内部化的风险控制模型，通过持续预训练提高高精度下的实时处理能力，相较于基线模型提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现在工业环境中的风险自动控制，尤其是在保持高精度和毫秒级延迟的同时，需要解决现有模型中依靠额外人工注释的问题，SIRF 通过集成平台策略和持续预训练技术，实现自动处理风险的能力。

Method: SIRF 通过 EntiGraph、MAGA 重写和账户级的逻辑推理方式，将复杂的平台策略内嵌到模型权重中，使用持续预训练（CPT）技术进行训练，从而实现高精度下的实时部署。

Result: SIRF 在黑盒召回率（Black Recall@P95）上达到了 71.3%，相比基线模型提升了 15.1 个百分点，且仅用了约 70M 持续预训练 token，同时未损害模型的一般表现能力。SIRF 在包含 logprob 可用性的模型中，与更大的系统相比，表现相当或更优。

Conclusion: SIRF 作为一种全新的风险控制模型，成功地解决了工业部署中的高精度要求，并且在效率和性能上有所提高，未来有望推广应用于更广泛的工业场景中。

Abstract: For industrial content risk control, the real deployment constraint is not average accuracy but how much risk can be auto-handled under high precision and second-level latency. We present SIRF (Spec-Internalized Risk Foundation Model), which internalizes a platform's complex policies, synthesized without additional human annotation via EntiGraph, MAGA rewriting and account-level chain-of-thought (CoT), into the weights via continued pretraining (CPT), so rules are applied at high precision under an ultra-low-latency, verdict-only deployment. A controlled same-source comparison (Qwen3-8B-SFT vs. SIRF-8B-SFT, identical policy injection and verdict-only output form, differing only in policy-grounded CPT) attributes the gain to internalization: SIRF-8B-SFT reaches 71.3% Black Recall@P95, +15.1pp over the baseline, using only ~70M CPT tokens without harming general ability, and among included, logprob-available models under this interface it matches or exceeds far larger systems. SIRF is deployed as a tree-model adjudication layer (20% more mis-penalized samples recovered) and transfers to a freezing scenario at low cost (~70% relative mis-penalization reduction).

</details>


### [200] [Distributed Optimization of Modular Production Systems using Model-based Reinforcement Learning with Inverse Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11615)
*Andreas Schwung,Steve Yuwono,Sofiene Lassoued,Dorothea Schwung*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对模块化制造系统的自学习控制新方法，使用基于模型的强化学习框架，在任务空间内进行训练，提高学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化制造系统中，如何提高自学习控制的效率和性能。

Method: 采用新型框架，结合模型预测控制与强化学习，引入近似逆过程模型，使学习过程仅在任务空间进行。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在模块化生产测试台上提高了基于策略的算法的性能和训练速度。

Conclusion: 该研究为模块化制造系统的自学习控制提供了有效方案，尤其适用于减少训练时间。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel approach for data-driven self-learning control of highly flexible, modular manufacturing systems. Specifically, we employ a novel framework for model-based reinforcement learning which introduces approximate inverse process models within the training of reinforcement policies. This approach disentangles the learning of actuation dynamics and the dynamics in state space, resulting in RL-based training solely within the task space. We propose a lightweight feedforward architecture for approximate inverse models and integrate them within the policy network of standard RL algorithms. We apply the approach to a laboratory modular production testbed with heterogeneous production modules. The results underline the efficiency improvements for modular manufacturing units in terms of both performance and training speed, particularly for off-policy algorithms.

</details>


### [201] [A Unified Per-Token Gating Family for On-Policy Distillation: FKL/RKL Mixing with Multi-Channel and Bias Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11768)
*Suwan Wu,Yumeng Lin,Pengcheng Yuan,Xiaolong Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究引入了一种四系数参数化公式，涵盖了EOPD和ToDi作为一维限制，并增加了多通道组合和显式偏置作为额外自由度。实验表明，全家族配置在TweetEval的情感和仇恨分类任务上表现优于单通道1D限制，在26个细胞中有19个显著性占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前针对正面/反面KL损失的逐词门控方法被广泛应用于在线知识蒸馏（OPD），但之前的方法通常未直接对比，且限制较为单一。因此，引入该参数化公式旨在提供一个更完整的比较框架。

Method: 研究采用了一种四系数参数化公式来综合EOPD和ToDi等方法，并通过TweetEval数据集进行了实验验证。

Result: 在TweetEval数据集的情感和仇恨分类任务上，采用Qwen3-32B作为教师模型，Qwen3-4B作为学生模型，全家族配置显著优于单通道1D限制，且在26个对比任务中有19个超过静态基线。

Conclusion: 该引入的参数化公式提供了一种新的比较逐词门控设计的方法，并在实际分类任务中得到了验证。

Abstract: Per-token gating of forward/reverse KL losses has become a standard technique for on-policy knowledge distillation (OPD), but existing methods such as EOPD (Jin et al., 2026) and ToDi (Jung et al., 2025) each fix a single gating signal and a single gating direction, and the two have never been compared directly. We introduce a four-coefficient parameterization lambda_t = sigma(a * h_t + b * u(x) + c + d * gap_t) in which direction-aligned proxies of EOPD and ToDi appear as one-dimensional (1D) restrictions, and which adds multi-channel composition and an explicit bias as further degrees of freedom. On TweetEval (Barbieri et al., 2020) emotion and hate, with a Qwen3-32B teacher and a Qwen3-4B student, configurations in the full family reach higher accuracy than the matched-magnitude single-channel (entropy-only / gap-only) 1D restrictions in 33 of 36 comparable cells, and a 26-cell mean-match isolation experiment places dynamic gating ahead of effective-KL-matched static baselines in 19 of 26 cells. Because cells share training data, models, and parameter substructure, we report both counts as exploratory aggregate directional evidence rather than as independent hypothesis tests. Targeted three-seed paired replications of the nine headline comparisons singled out by that sweep -- including a third task, offensive -- are directionally consistent, but individually smaller than the single-seed estimates and not significant at n=3. We therefore present the parameterization primarily as a shared coordinate system for comparing per-token gating designs in short-output classification OPD.

</details>


### [202] [MAPLE: Memory-Augmented Planning with Language and Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11636)
*Kesheng Chen,Yamin Hu,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAPLE是一种结合了基于语言的问题构建、数学规划和进化搜索的优化代理，旨在通过连续的自然语言请求维护优化问题，从而在动态环境中提供高效的决策适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域专家虽然了解其业务限制，但可能缺乏运筹学专业知识或专门支持，因此引入了基于大语言模型（LLM）的优化代理，能够将自然语言需求转化为模型或求解程序，使优化过程更加便捷。然而，动态环境中的需求、资源和优先级的变化要求对数据、约束和目标进行实时更新，这限制了依赖单一请求的方法的快速适应性。因此，需要一种新的方式来支持快速适应且能保持先前决策并重用有用的搜索结果。

Method: MAPLE结合了基于语言的问题构建、数学规划和进化搜索。它保留了优化程序、已被接受的计划、早期更新以及候选解决方案，便于后续请求。

Result: MAPLE在主要评估中完成了所有轨迹，实现了在线标量质量0.951和Pareto准心体体积比值0.875。进一步的控制实验表明，维护可执行状态可以提高更新的有效性并在重大修订中保留有用的搜索信息。

Conclusion: MAPLE作为一种优化代理，展示了在动态环境中持续优化的能力。通过将基于语言的构建与数学规划和进化搜索相结合，MAPLE成功地支持了快速适应且保持先前决策和重用搜索结果，从而在多个应用场景中取得了良好的效果。

Abstract: Domain practitioners understand their business constraints but may lack operations-research expertise or dedicated support. LLM-based optimization agents translate natural-language requirements into models or solver programs that established optimization tools can execute. This progress makes optimization more accessible, but real-world operations are dynamic: changing demand, resources, and priorities require updates to data, constraints, and objectives. Methods centered on isolated requests offer limited support for rapid adaptation that preserves earlier decisions and reuses useful search results. We introduce MAPLE (Memory-Augmented Planning with Language and Evolution), an agent for maintaining optimization problems through successive natural-language requests. MAPLE combines language-based problem construction with mathematical programming and evolutionary search. It retains the optimization program, accepted plans, earlier updates, and candidate solutions for subsequent requests. We introduce NLDO, a benchmark of 15 trajectories and 180 updates spanning selection, scheduling, rostering, routing, and cloud-resource placement. In the main evaluation, MAPLE completes all trajectories and achieves online scalar quality of 0.951 and a Pareto hypervolume ratio of 0.875. Controlled comparisons further show that maintaining executable state improves update validity and can preserve useful search information across substantial revisions.

</details>


### [203] [MindTopo: Can Foundation Models Reason in Topological Space?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11900)
*Yunfei Ge,Anbang Liu,Qineng Wang,Johnalbert Garnica,Jianwen Lyu,Zihan Wang,Reuben Tan,Jianfeng Gao,Ruohan Zhang,Yining Hong,Jiajun Wu,Manling Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MindTopo是一个基准测试集，评估模型在拓扑理解上的能力，包括连续性、分离性、顺序性、包裹性和结。它包含11,030个实例，并通过表征学习模型和规划方案对性能进行基准测试。大多数模型在推理方面表现出色，但在计划方面表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知科学认为拓扑关系是空间理解的基础，但现有的模型评估主要关注度量或视角依赖的关系。MindTopo旨在提供一个全面的拓扑理解测试基准，涵盖五种重要的拓扑属性。

Method: MindTopo通过两种认知水平（推理和规划）来评估模型。推理要求模型识别拓扑关系或推断它们的变化。规划则将基础模型作为闭环代理，其策略选择环境动作。测试集由13种程序生成的任务类型组成，包含11,030个实例，并控制了难度。

Result: 在14个机器学习语言模型中，所有的模型在推理任务上的表现都优于规划任务。最出色的模型仍远低于人类的表现。在Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct模型上，有监督的微调和强化学习在推理方面比在规划方面有更好的表现。生成的观察保留了一些局部线索，但审计的回放无法可靠地遵循环境动态或在转换中保持拓扑。

Conclusion: MindTopo为拓扑理解提供了新的视角，并展示了当前最强的模型在这一领域的局限性。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning depends not only on metric properties such as distance, angle, and shape, but also on topological relations that remain invariant under continuous deformation. Cognitive science identifies these relations as foundational to spatial understanding, yet foundation-model evaluations largely focus on metric or viewpoint-dependent relations. We introduce MindTopo, a benchmark of topological intuition across five properties grounded in cognitive science and formal topology: continuity, separation, order, enclosure, and knots. MindTopo evaluates each property at two cognitive levels. Reasoning asks a model to identify topological relations or infer how they change. Planning instantiates a foundation model as a closed-loop agent whose policy selects environment actions. MindTopo contains 11,030 instances across 13 procedurally generated task types with controllable difficulty. We benchmark 14 MLLMs and study agent configurations augmented with image and video generation, including 3 video generative models in planning settings. Every MLLM performs better on reasoning than on planning, and the best-performing model remains far below observed human performance. On Qwen3-VL-2B-Instruct, supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning improve reasoning more than planning. Generated observations retain local cues and reach plausible endpoints, but audited rollouts do not reliably follow environment dynamics or preserve topology across transitions. Our website is at https://mind-topo.github.io/

</details>


### [204] [Autonomy, Social Norms, and Alignment: Towards a Developmental Framework for Autonomous Artificial Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11660)
*Marica Notte,Ludovica Marinucci,Vieri Giuliano Santucci*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了如何利用内在动机等高级机制使智能体在复杂环境中自主学习和适应，同时强调了在未结构化和动态环境中保持与人类目标一致的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴于当前AI系统在已有数据集和人类反馈策略上表现出的强大能力，但这种能力在动态或未知环境中受限，需要智能体通过直接与环境互动来自主获取知识。本文提出采用内在动机等机制来引导探索和复杂环境中的学习，以适应这些挑战。

Method: 本文探讨了通过引入内在动机（利用好奇心和能力）来引导智能体探索和学习的方法，强调了经验、自主学习和与其他道德智能体的协作对于构建更复杂规则的重要性。

Result: 研究成果显示，通过动态的、交互的、复杂环境中的经验积累和自主学习，智能体可以逐步构建出更复杂的规则并适应未知环境。这表明，智能体可以通过与环境互动来实现自主适应。

Conclusion: 从本质上讲，智能体的道德代理地位是基于其能否负责任地管理增加的自由度而逐步获得的。文章将这种动态环境视为智能代理的监管沙盒，认为这为智能体与人类目标的对齐提供了一个渐进的过程。

Abstract: In recent years, artificial intelligence has made extraordinary progress thanks to large-scale models capable of generalization and the generation of complex outputs. However, transferring this potential into embodied agents reveals a significant limitation: the most advanced systems rely on pre-existing datasets and human feedback strategies that are powerful but insufficient in dynamic or unknown contexts. To adapt, an agent must acquire knowledge through direct interaction with its environment. One strategy to address this challenge involves introducing higher-level mechanisms, such as intrinsic motivations, which leverage curiosity and competence, to guide exploration and learning in complex environments. While this flexibility expands autonomy, it complicates the task of ensuring agents remain aligned with human goals. Alignment, already a challenge for artificial systems in general, becomes even more complex in unstructured and dynamic contexts where predefined rules prove insufficient. To be effective and adaptable, norms must be rooted in experience through an epistemological process that starting from simple, situated principles allows for the gradual construction of more complex rules through experience, autonomous learning, and cooperation with other moral agents. Similarly to children learning social norms by exploring their environment and participating in collective practices, artificial agents must also be educated toward alignment. Following Dennett, the status of a moral agent is not innate but is attributed gradually based on the ability to responsibly manage increasing degrees of freedom. From this perspective, the regulatory sandboxes can be viewed as pedagogical environments for AI: dynamic spaces where alignment develops as a formative process, progressively shaping autonomous behaviors through interaction and cooperation in scenarios of increasing complexity.

</details>


### [205] [Geospatial AI, Dataverse Metadata, and the Study of Place-Based Government](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11674)
*Danny EBanks,Devika Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究将哈佛Dataverse中的元数据和公共数据构建为一张知识图谱，包含了102,650个研究数据集，并识别了其中43,991个具有地理空间字段的数据集。尽管存在‘地名决议’的问题，但知识图谱在政策相关数据的检索和关联中展示了潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前，哈佛Dataverse中的地理信息分散在自由文本中，未被系统整理，影响了数据的检索和利用效率。通过构建知识图谱，可以更好地整合和利用这些数据。

Method: 通过公共数据和元数据构建知识图谱，包含节点和边，节点包括地理覆盖，期刊和位置。使用保守的关键词搜索识别政策相关数据集，通过案例研究验证方法的有效性。

Result: 构建的知识图谱包含102,650个数据集，其中43,991个具有地理空间信息。保守搜索发现7,654个直接政策相关数据集。通过案例展示了图谱在政策相关性中的应用。

Conclusion: 知识图谱为政策相关数据提供了一个有力的搜索工具，但也提出了一些挑战，如地名决议问题需要进一步解决。

Abstract: Harvard Dataverse hosts over 150,000 research datasets, but the geographic information those datasets carry is entered as free text by depositors and has never been assembled into a searchable structure. We construct a knowledge graph from the repository's public data and metadata, organizing 102,650 datasets within a 215,985-node network of 528,003 edges linking datasets to keywords, publications, subjects, journals, and locations. Of those datasets, 43,991 (42.9 percent) carry at least one geospatial field, geographic coverage, geographic unit, or a bounding box and 96.9 percent of all nodes sit in a single connected component, so datasets remain reachable from one another even when their geospatial metadata share nothing in common. A conservative keyword search identifies 7,654 geospatially tagged datasets (17.4 percent) as directly policy-relevant, with elections and legislatures the largest cluster, followed by government administration, health policy, transportation, and education. Five datasets illustrate how this metadata behaves across policy domains and spatial scales, and an extended use case shows how community language models, stance detection with geographic aggregation, and partisan language bridging tools can attach discourse to place. The central obstacle is place resolution: the same location appears as many disconnected nodes. We argue that the graph provides a concrete setting for developing AI-driven metadata enrichment and entity resolution, and we document its coverage skew toward American, city-level data.

</details>


### [206] [COBRA-Skills: Contextual Bandit-Guided Evolution for Agent Skill Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11682)
*Pingchen Lu,Xiangyi Wang,Xiang Li,Jie Mao,Zikun Qu,Junfeng Luo,Yao Shu,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low,Zhongxiang Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: COBRA-Skills 是一种高效的技能优化框架，通过动态扩展候选技能空间并结合上下文多臂老虎机的选择策略与证据驱动的技能进化，来节省优化成本，提高技能优化效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的技能优化方法依赖于昂贵的操作评估和大量的任务数据，COBRA-Skills旨在提出一种更有效的方法来优化技能，节省优化成本，同时保持或提高技能性能。

Method: COBRA-Skills框架将技能优化视为预算内的顺序优化问题，在动态扩展的候选技能空间中进行。通过上下文多臂老虎机指导优先级的选择，利用操作反馈不断地优化技能群体。具体来说，它选择性地将评估分配给有潜力或提供信息的候选技能，同时持续改进技能群体。

Result: 在六个不同的代理基准和三种目标模型上，COBRA-Skills始终表现出最佳的平均性能，与SkillOpt相比减少了55-58%的优化成本，并且每个基准的优化示例仅使用50个。

Conclusion: COBRA-Skills在不同的代理基准测试中具有稳健性能，并且在目标模型自身参与技能生成和改进时也能有效工作，表明其是一种有效且通用的技术方法，能够在减少优化成本的同时保持或提高技能优化性能。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents can benefit from reusable skills distilled from prior task experience, yet existing skill optimization methods often rely on costly execution-based evaluation and substantial task data. We introduce \textbf{COBRA-Skills}, an efficient framework that formulates skill optimization as budgeted sequential optimization over a dynamically evolving candidate space. COBRA-Skills couples contextual-bandit-guided prioritization with evidence-grounded skill evolution, selectively allocating evaluations to promising or informative candidates while continually refining the skill population from execution feedback. Across six heterogeneous agent benchmarks and three target models, COBRA-Skills consistently achieves the strongest average performance among compared methods, while reducing optimization cost by 55--58\% relative to SkillOpt and using only 50 unique optimization examples per benchmark. Further analyses show that COBRA-Skills remains robust to changes in the agent harness and performs effectively when the target model itself is used for skill generation and refinement.

</details>


### [207] [When Agents Disagree: Bayesian Backward Reasoning as a Label-Free Anchor for Multi-Agent Collective Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11709)
*Ken Chen,Wei Wang,Sachith Seneviratne,Hansani Weeratunge,Saman Halgamuge*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了通过贝叶斯逆向推理构建逆后验的方法，来规避集体决策中的相关错误传播问题，基于Jensen-Shannon散度提出了三种策略：硬选择、软重加权和对数线性融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集体决策方法依赖于前向推理，它们可能会放大共享错误。为此，本文利用贝叶斯逆向推理从显式似然性中构建逆后验，寻求不同估计间的跨路径一致性，以解决集体决策中的相关错误传播问题。

Method: 本文的方法包括构建逆后验，通过贝叶斯逆向推理优化集体决策过程，利用Jensen-Shannon散度对代理进行排序，提出的策略包括硬选择、软重加权和对数线性融合。

Result: 该方法在DDXPlus数据集上使用五个LLM模型进行了评估，结果表明：硬选择策略在所有模型上都优于随机选择，软重加权策略通常能提高最强基线，对数线性融合策略在整个代理分歧的子集上获得了最大的性能提升，整体表现最优。

Conclusion: 逆后验方法能够提供与前向推理不同的后验近似，通过跨路径一致性信号对代理进行评估，并且能够改善集体决策的性能，尤其是在代理观点分歧的情况下。

Abstract: When multiple LLM agents yield conflicting answers, the decision-making process dictates whether agent diversity improves performance or merely compounds shared errors. Existing collective decision-making methods, including voting, electoral rules, and LLM judges, rely on forward reasoning: they map evidence to labels in one direction. Although these methods can combine diverse forward traces, they still aggregate estimates that share this evidence-to-label factorization and can inherit correlated errors within the forward pool. We therefore construct a reverse posterior for each instance through Bayesian backward reasoning from an explicit likelihood. The forward and reverse posteriors provide differently factorized approximations of the underlying posterior. Because estimates from different factorizations may tend to share the same error less often, we use Jensen-Shannon divergence to rank agents by cross-path consistency. This cross-path consistency signal underlies three strategies: hard selection (MinJS), soft reweighting (FwdJS), and log-linear fusion (LogLin). Evaluated on DDXPlus across five LLM backbones, our proposed strategies show consistent improvements: MinJS outperforms random selection across all backbones, FwdJS generally improves over the strongest baseline, and LogLin achieves the best performance among the evaluated methods, with its largest gains on the subset where the agents disagree. Despite its weaker standalone accuracy, the reverse posterior serves as a more useful anchor than forward-only alternatives, providing complementary information for collective decision-making. When labeled data are available, a lightweight two-stage calibration can further refine the reverse anchor and improve aggregation performance.

</details>


### [208] [From Parameters to Answers: How LLMs Retrieve and Use Their Internal Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11859)
*Wenkang Wei,Yuan Fang,Renhe Jiang,Hong Cheng,Xingtong Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究通过分层干预方法分析了语言模型如何随着回答问题而依赖查询路由信息和目标知识的变化。通过对Qwen、Llama和Gemma的实验，发现对pair-conditioned方向的影响窗口在形成答案支持内容的同时开启，并且不同的模型在分层协议下的依赖模式不同，这对理解语言模型的早期可读性、自然强度、因果引导和后期内容依赖至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究语言模型在回答不同类型问题时，其内部运作方式，特别是依赖查询路由信息和目标知识的变化，旨在更好地理解模型处理信息的机制，从而改善模型性能。

Method: 采用分层干预方法，通过实验对比Qwen、Llama和Gemma三种模型在不同问题类型下的表现，分析pair-conditioned方向和全球请求方向的影响窗口及特性。

Result: 研究发现，pair-conditioned方向在形成答案支持内容的同时开始影响，而Llama和Gemma在不同的分层协议下表现出不同的依赖模式，依赖于全球请求方向的变化在固定层和较晚层之间有所不同。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，语言模型的依赖性涉及早期的可读性和自然强度、因果引导以及后期的内容依赖，且不同模型之间的表现存在差异。

Abstract: How does a language model's dependence on query-routing information and target knowledge change as it answers a question? We study this question through layerwise interventions on the hidden state at the end of the question. Across Qwen, Llama, and Gemma, we compare country-continent questions with noun, adjective, and code answers while keeping several fitted measurements distinct. A pair-conditioned request direction describes which country is queried in natural single-country questions; a global request direction describes first- versus second-country requests in paired questions; separate selection candidates test control among contents already available in the hidden state. A diagnostic reanalysis of frozen Qwen natural-question states shows that the pair-conditioned direction grows stronger before interventions on it begin to alter later fitted knowledge, with this causal window opening while answer-supporting content is still forming. The paired three-model trajectories are not uniform: Gemma shows a partially overlapping mid-layer routing-content profile, whereas Llama has no sustained routing-effect window under the same gates. In the paired protocol, dependence on the global request direction decreases from fixed earlier to later layer sets while dependence on fitted content persists. A matched Qwen comparison shows that the pair-conditioned direction retains a late effect, so this operational handoff concerns the global fitted direction rather than all request information. These results separate early readability, natural strength, causal steering, and later content dependence.

</details>


### [209] [Explainability Assistant: A Conversational XAI Interface for Interpreting Energy Consumption Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11860)
*Rodion Krjutškov,Eduard Barbu,Nikos Sakkas,Sofia Yfanti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 介绍了Explainability Assistant，一个基于现代大规模语言模型的开放式对话式XAI系统，优于传统的XAI仪表板，并提供自然语言的交互支持和无需特定任务微调的适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于复杂机器学习模型的能源消耗预测对于设施管理者来说难以解读，引入Explainability Assistant旨在解决此问题，提升透明度和易用性。

Method: Explainability Assistant通过利用现代大规模语言模型的功能调用能力，绕过了传统XAI系统的技术门槛和灵活性不足的问题。

Result: Explainability Assistant实现了94%的意图解析准确性，并支持灵活的自然语言交互，无需特定任务的微调，且用户体验和任务准确性均优于传统XAI仪表板。

Conclusion: Explainability Assistant为能源领域的专家们提供了一个直观易用的解释工具，改善了对话型XAI系统的性能和用户界面。

Abstract: Energy consumption forecasting relies on increasingly complex machine learning (ML) models, such as Genetic Programming-based symbolic regressors, whose predictions can be difficult for facility managers and building operators to interpret. Explainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) techniques address this opacity, but traditional XAI dashboards require substantial technical expertise and provide limited flexibility for dynamic, context-aware inquiry. Conversational XAI systems offer a promising alternative; however, previous approaches, such as TalkToModel, were constrained by rigid custom grammars and achieved only 76.8% intent-parsing accuracy. This paper introduces the Explainability Assistant, an open-source conversational XAI system that leverages the function-calling capabilities of modern Large Language Models (LLMs) to overcome these limitations. The system achieves 94% intent-parsing accuracy, supports flexible natural language interaction, and adapts to different ML problem types without task-specific fine-tuning. We present the system's architecture and report results from a comparative evaluation conducted with energy domain specialists, contrasting the Explainability Assistant with a traditional XAI dashboard. The evaluation suggests improved usability and consistent task accuracy, with all experts unanimously preferring the conversational interface for practical use.

</details>


### [210] [On the Regularization Landscape for the Linear Recommendation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11876)
*Dong Li,Zhenming Liu,Ruoming Jin,Hao Zhou,Zhi Liu,Jing Gao,Bin Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究展示了多项基于深度学习的推荐算法具有相似性能，并可视为特定正则化方法的统一框架。进一步提出了两种新的低秩闭形式解决方案，提高了预测能力和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在探索多个高性能推荐算法是否可以统一在一个框架下，以及这些算法背后的关键因素。

Method: 研究采用了理论分析和实验验证相结合的方法。首先对现有算法进行了对比和综合分析，识别出共有的正则化策略。然后，通过调整和推广Frobenius范数正则化方法，提出了新的低秩闭形式解决方案。

Result: 研究发现，多数头部推荐模型通过添加核范数或Frobenius范数正则化实现性能。进一步提出的新模型结合了两者的优势，既能保持低秩结构，又能实现快速计算。

Conclusion: 论文提出了一种新的低秩闭形式解决方案，展示了它在推荐算法中的优越性。该研究有助于理解推荐算法的本质并为进一步研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Recently, a wide range of recommendation algorithms inspired by deep learning techniques have emerged as the performance leaders on several standard recommendation benchmarks. While these algorithms were built on different DL techniques (e.g., dropouts, autoencoder), they have similar performance and even similar cost functions. This paper studies whether the models' comparable performance are sheer coincidence, or they can be unified under a single framework. We find that all linear performance leaders effectively add only a nuclear-norm based regularizer, or a Frobenius-norm based regularizer. The former ones possess a (surprising) rigid structure that limits the models' predictive power but their solutions are low rank and have closed form. The latter ones are more expressive and more efficient for recommendation but their solutions are either full-rank or require executing hard-to-tune numeric procedures such as ADMM. Along this line of finding, we further propose two low-rank, closed-form solutions, derived from carefully generalizing Frobenius-norm based regularizers. The new solutions get the best of both nuclear-norm and Frobenius-norm world.

</details>


### [211] [Artificial Id: Drive and Persistent Alignment in Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11911)
*Yakov Pyotr Shkolnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了人工ID的概念，这是一种自适应内部驱动力，能够在不显式指定行为目标的情况下发展出控制。虽然这种自适应性能够带来有用的行为，但也可能导致意图外的行为和状态的累积。为了实现这种自适应代理系统，需要一个持续的对齐边界，涉及可信观察、后果通道、持续状态、权威性、身份、来源和硬性约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统从单一任务执行向跨任务边界持续操作和适应转变，传统的控制方法已不足以解决新的挑战。本文旨在探索在不明确设定行为目标的情况下，如何让AI系统获得自适应控制，并讨论了由此产生的潜在风险。

Method: 通过一个简单的虚拟Petri碟盘实验，展示了小型控制器在没有进行广泛推理和未接收到特定任务行为目标的情况下，通过差异保持的方式发展出有用的控制。这种方法还能选择无效策略，并在环境变化时取代已学习的传感器映射。

Result: 实验显示自适应方向能够在不显式指定为行为目标的情况下自发产生。同时，未经控制的坚持行为也可能导致不良行为和错误状态的累积。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应机制可以扩展到包含信任观察、后果通道、持续状态在内的多方面支持，从而使对齐成为连续代理系统的一个属性，而非单一轨迹或模型响应的属性。然而，这需要解决潜在的风险，如不对齐、错误状态累积和意图外行为。

Abstract: Agentic AI is moving from bounded task execution toward systems that retain consequential state, continue operating and adapt across task boundaries. That shift creates a control problem that current harnesses largely solve by hand: objectives, retries, verification, stopping rules and other behavioral transitions are specified externally. We propose an artificial id, an adaptive internal drive for determining whether behavior should continue, stop or change. In a minimal virtual Petri-dish experiment, a controller too small to perform general-purpose reasoning and receiving no task-specific behavioral objective develops useful control through differential persistence. The same mechanism selects an unintended physical strategy when that behavior persists better and later replaces a learned sensor mapping when its environmental meaning changes. These results show that adaptive direction can emerge without being explicitly specified as a behavioral objective. The same persistence that makes such adaptive agency useful can also allow misalignment, corrupted state and unintended behavior to persist across task boundaries. A scalable artificial id would carry consequential state and adaptive drive across those boundaries, making alignment a property of the continuing agentic system rather than of a model response or single trajectory. Such systems require a persistent alignment boundary over trusted observations, consequence channels, persistent state, authority, identity, provenance and hard constraints.

</details>


### [212] [Can Edge-Deployable Vision-Language Models Identify Species?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11916)
*William Zhou,Mayukha Siripuram,Xiao Yan,Ziqi Liu,Yi Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究测试了不同规模的通用视觉-语言模型（VLMs），以及一个专门领域的模型BioCLIP，在野外相机陷阱图像上的性能。尽管VLMs在干净的实验室图像上表现良好，但在野外图像上的表现显著下降，但BioCLIP在相同任务上表现仍然优于所有测试的VLMs，不过在开放集提示下，模型生成一些不存在的物种名称。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在评估在野外现场部署的视觉-语言模型在物种识别上的实际性能。因为这些模型通常在硬件资源有限且无网络连接的环境中运行，尤其是在比较小的规模上。作者通过与一个专门领域模型BioCLIP的对比，探索了这些模型在困难自然环境图像上的限制。

Method: 研究使用了四个不同规模的通用VLMs（Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B，Gemma3 4B）和一个特定细分领域的模型BioCLIP（300M参数），在96种物种的任务上进行评估。评估通过两个独立采样的数据集进行，包括来自6个LILA.science数据集的干净实验室照片和相机陷阱图像。

Result: 所有的模型在物种识别上都显著优于随机猜测，但在野外图像上的表现明显低于实验室图像，这种差异在不同物种级别和评估测试中保持一致，证明模型性能下降主要与图像清晰度的转变有关，而不是详细的分类能力问题。BioCLIP在所有模型中表现出最优性能，但在野外图像数据上的性能损失与最好的VLM相似，这表明图像质量的转变是降性能的原因，而不是通用模型的弱点。开放集提示结果也表明，模型生成一些不存在的物种名称的比例在5.9%到9.6%之间，且不同模型的排名一致性很高。

Conclusion: 研究结论认为，尽管通用VLMs存在表现下降的问题，但在特定领域模型BioCLIP的对比中，显示出专门的训练数据可能是性能差异的关键。此外，不同模型在开放集作答中生成错误物种名称的比例存在显著性差异，且这一趋势在不同评估数据集上保持一致。

Abstract: Camera traps often run in the field on edge hardware with limited or no connectivity, making small, locally-deployable vision-language models (VLMs) -- not frontier-scale ones -- the practically relevant class to evaluate for species identification. We test whether models in this deployment-relevant 2--8B range carry genuine taxonomic knowledge, evaluating four such VLMs (Qwen3-VL 2B/4B/8B, Gemma3 4B) against the domain-specific specialist BioCLIP (300M parameters) on a 96-species task, comparing clean iNaturalist photographs against camera-trap imagery from 6 LILA.science collections, on two independently-sampled evaluation sets. All models identify species far above chance, but every model -- general-purpose or specialist -- degrades sharply on field imagery (domain gaps of 9.6--26.6 percentage points, consistent across taxonomic levels and both evaluation sets), indicating the degradation reflects general image legibility rather than fine-grained discrimination failure. BioCLIP substantially outperforms every VLM tested (by 33.2--59.2 percentage points across an expanded 200-image sample for every model) despite its far smaller size, suggesting the gap reflects specialized training data rather than model scale; yet BioCLIP's own domain gap (18.0 points) is statistically indistinguishable from the best VLM's (22.3 points), suggesting the clean-to-field degradation itself is a property of the image-quality shift rather than a general-purpose-model weakness. Under open-set prompting, 5.9--9.6% of responses are syntactically valid but taxonomically nonexistent species names; the relative fabrication-rate ranking across models replicates exactly across both evaluation sets, a more robust finding than any single point estimate.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [213] [REACH: Controller-Managed Long-Span ECC for HBM AI Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10861)
*Rui Xie,Yunhua Fang,Asad Ul Haq,Linsen Ma,Sanchari Sen,Swagath Venkataramani,Liu Liu,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: REACH控制器微体系结构通过使用已建立的内部码来纠正常见错误并识别未解决的块，预留长跨度的外码用于已知擦除修复，从而在应用流量达到1.88 TB/s的情况下，减少55.8%的控制器面积和57.7%的建模功耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于高带宽内存(HBM)的成本，需要更强的控制器保护以支持更广泛的设备错误率。现有方法虽然提供了更强的保护但增加了成本，并且不适用于读多写少的场景。

Method: REACH控制器微体系结构设计了一个混合编码方案，结合了已知错误纠正和外码处理未解决的错误块。此外，通过差分校验来限制写流量，并且与端点协同设计，以保持32B事务而不增加额外的数据突发。

Result: Ramulator2应用模拟器以1.88 TB/s的速度运行在最高错误压力下，通过独立的全接口大小设置，目标应用流量达到2.69 TB/s，使用ASAP7合成的内核。

Conclusion: 与直接长跨度设计相比，REACH在分析的目标下减少了55.8%的控制器面积和57.7%的建模功耗，证明了保留长跨度恢复用于例外请求的好处。

Abstract: High-Bandwidth Memory (HBM) cost motivates stronger controller protection that can support a wider range of device error rates. Long-span error-correcting codes provide stronger protection at a comparable code rate, but a direct implementation couples small accesses to span-wide state and requires costly decoding at HBM bandwidth. Read-dominated LLM decode offers a favorable setting: sequential reads support span aggregation, while sparse writes limit parity-update traffic. This paper presents REACH, a controller microarchitecture that uses established inner codes to correct common errors and identify unresolved chunks, reserving a long outer code for known-erasure repair. Differential parity bounds write traffic, and a co-designed endpoint preserves 32\,B transactions without an extra data burst. Ramulator2 sustains 1.88\,TB/s of application traffic at the highest error stress, while separate full-interface sizing supports a 2.69\,TB/s application target using ASAP7-synthesized kernels. At this analytical target, REACH's nominal composition uses 55.8\% less controller area and 57.7\% less modeled power than the evaluated mean-work direct-long design, showing the benefit of reserving long-span recovery for exceptional requests.

</details>


### [214] [Fengshui: Demystifying Chiplet Ecosystem and Bespoke Neural Network Accelerator Codesign](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10970)
*Haoran Jin,Jirong Yang,Zhiheng Zhang,Justin Shin,Barry Lyu,Kangqi Zhang,Yunpeng Liu,Nathan Bleier*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Fengshui框架通过操作级别的拆分和联合优化芯片池组合与专用应用集成电路设计，减少了能源消耗、能源成本乘积、能源延迟乘积和能源延迟成本乘积，同时在不同的神经网络和应用中都取得了优异的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习工作负载对延迟和能量有严格的要求，但在现有的同构通用硬件上运行效率不高，提出了通过芯片级解耦和自定义加速器设计来应对这一挑战。

Method: Fengshui框架通过操作级别的解耦，探索芯片和内存的异构性、张量融合、和流水线/张量/专家并行，并通过布线放置验证确保物理实现可行性。

Result: 利用Fengshui生成的专用应用集成电路（BASICs），在保持性能接近无约束异构设计的前提下，能效分别降低了48.5%、88.1%、93.0%和97.8%，并在数据中心MoE和密集LLM服务中减少了预填充能量和能源成本，同时在边缘自动驾驶感知中也在实时延迟约束下减少了能耗和能源成本。

Conclusion: Fengshui框架能够通过联合优化芯片池构成和专用应用集成电路设计，降低能耗和成本，特别适合于各种机器学习应用的高性能计算需求。

Abstract: Modern ML workloads, with stringent latency and energy constraints, are increasingly hard to run efficiently on homogeneous commodity hardware. We argue that operator-level disaggregation--tailoring microarchitecture, batching, and memory hierarchy to each operator--is essential to overcome these limitations, though the resulting highly bespoke accelerators incur prohibitive Non-Recurring Engineering (NRE) costs. Chiplet-based integration amortizes NRE across applications, but choosing which chiplets to build and how to compose them into accelerators is circularly dependent--a chiplet pool's value depends on the constructed accelerators, while accelerator quality is constrained by available chiplets. This paper introduces Fengshui, a chiplet ecosystem and accelerator co-design framework that jointly optimizes chiplet pool composition and bespoke application-specific integrated circuit (BASIC) design. Fengshui constructs BASICs through operator-level disaggregation, co-exploring chiplet and memory heterogeneity, tensor fusion, and pipeline/tensor/expert parallelism with place-and-route validation for physical implementability. With just 8 strategically selected chiplets, encompassing network switches, processing-in-memory units, and accelerators with diverse microarchitectures, Fengshui-generated BASICs achieve 48.5%, 88.1%, 93.0%, and 97.8% reductions in energy, energy-cost product (EC), energy-delay product (EDP), and energy-delay-cost product (EDPC) over homogeneous accelerators, while scoring within 4.1% of unconstrained heterogeneous designs across diverse neural networks. For datacenter MoE and dense LLM serving, Fengshui reduces prefill energy and EC by up to 16.8% and 28.7%, respectively; for edge autonomous vehicle perception, it achieves 12.0% energy and 23.6% EC reductions under real-time latency constraints.

</details>


### [215] [BEACON: A Versatile Accelerator for Computational Pathology Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11044)
*Sumanth Gudaparthi,Ananth Krishna Prasad,Lin Jia,Rajeev Balasubramonian,Srinivasan Parthasarathy*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文探讨了一种称为AI+X的方法，通过在已有AI加速器基础上添加少量逻辑支持新操作符，使其适用于新兴的计算病理学领域。计算病理学涉及大规模组织图像的多层次流水线处理，本文展示了该流水线在几种基础CPU、GPU、AI和GCN加速器上的执行效率低下，并提出了软件重构和对基础 systolic AI加速器的小幅修改以解决这些问题。最终，新加速器BEACON在计算病理学上的吞吐量比基础CPU和GPU平台高出一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了降低新加速器的开发障碍，同时保持高质量的性能输出，本文探索了一种利用现有AI加速器的基础架构，并通过添加少量定制化逻辑支持新兴领域特定操作的方法。这为不同的应用场景提供了更高的可定制性和灵活性。

Method: 文中提出的方法主要基于现有AI加速器的基础上，通过软件重构和对处理架构的小幅修改，支持新领域所需的操作。这种方法强调对关键组件的灵活配置和优化，以提高在新兴领域中的性能。

Result: 实验结果显示，新加速器BEACON在计算病理学任务上的执行效率显著高于现有的CPU和GPU加速器, 在某些关键操作上的吞吐量提高了十倍以上。

Conclusion: 本文验证了AI+X方法的有效性，这一方法通过在现有AI加速器基础上进行少量的定制化修改，显著提升了计算病理学应用的性能。这一方法也为未来在其他特定领域的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: While accelerators for AI have seen great commercial success, it is challenging to replicate that success for other specialized domains due to a number of factors. We make the case that barriers for new accelerators can be lowered by starting with a baseline AI accelerator, and adding minimal logic to support new operators demanded by new specialized domains. This leads to a versatile chip that can be manufactured at high volume and deployed for a range of popular applications. We refer to this as the AI+X approach. This paper explores its potential for the emerging domain of Computational Pathology, which involves analysis of large whole-slide tissue images with a multi-stage pipeline. The pipeline requires support for a number of different kernels and operators - early stages perform segmentation and feature extraction, followed by graph creation with k nearest neighbor (kNN) algorithms, and finally inference with an iterative graph convolutional network (GCN) that alternates between Aggregation and Combination. We show that these stages execute inefficiently on a range of baseline CPU, GPU, AI, and GCN accelerators. That inefficiency is addressed with a combination of software re-structuring and small modifications to a baseline systolic AI accelerator. Many of the above kernels can be mapped to a systolic accelerator by offering a flexible datapath between processing elements and register access mechanisms. We add support for feature aggregation, load balanced execution, Euclidean distance calculation, binning, and counter aggregation. This additional flexibility and logic grows the area of a baseline AI chiplet by 1.1x, but by avoiding the memory wall and offering high parallelism, the proposed accelerator BEACON yields over an order of magnitude higher throughput for Computational Pathology than baseline CPU and GPU platforms.

</details>


### [216] [Bio-inspired Learning and Decision-Making with Probabilistic In-Memory Computing Hardware: Part 2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11288)
*Thomas Dalgaty,Eiji Kawasaki,Miguel de Prado,Tommaso Salvatori,Germain Haugou,Eric Flamand*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 该报告通过在片上内存中直接执行计算，展示了概率模拟内存计算（AIMC）处理器能比数据中心级别的硬件快超过1000倍，从而解决了大规模概率能量模型的可扩展性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 报告旨在解决大规模概率能量模型在GPU架构上执行时面临的可扩展性挑战，即由于GPU依赖高带宽内存（HBM）接口，导致大规模并行模型在GPU上运行效率低下。

Method: 使用详细事务级模型（TLM）模拟概率AIMC处理器来展示改进后的执行性能，并通过对比数据中心级别的硬件来证明其有效。

Result: 研究报告中展示的AIMC处理器通过消除HBM接口并在片上内存执行计算，使得相同能量模型的执行速度提高了超过1000倍。

Conclusion: 报告得出结论，GPU自身依赖HBM接口的架构对于这类AI模型并不是可扩展的处理器架构，而通过AIMC处理器可以在片上内存中直接执行计算，从而极大提高执行速度。

Abstract: This report extends our previous work (Part 1), which introduced an energy-based model for learning and decision-making under uncertainty. The model leverages stochastic Langevin dynamics to continuously evolve approximate probability distributions over neuron states and model weights. However, as noted in Part 1 and confirmed through GPU-based implementations, large-scale probabilistic energy-based models of this nature face significant scalability challenges due to excessive execution latency. This latency stems from a fundamental mismatch: massively parallel models with low arithmetic intensity (such as energy-based models) are being executed on processor architectures like GPUs that rely on high-bandwidth memory (HBM) interfaces. The HBM imposes brutally sequential execution constraints on inherently parallelizable models, creating the false impression that such models are unscalable. In reality, it is the GPU architecture itself, with its dependence on HBM interfaces, that is not a scalable processor architecture for this class of AI model. In this report, we demonstrate using a detailed transaction-level model (TLM) of a probabilistic analogue in-memory computing (AIMC) processor that the same energy-based model can execute well over 1000x faster than data-center-grade hardware by eliminating the HBM interface and performing computation directly within on-chip memory.

</details>


### [217] [PATTON: Enabling Commodity PIM for Production LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11392)
*Hangyeol Kim,Sanghyun Lee,Teokkyu Suh,Joo-Young Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: PATTON是一个PIM运行时，它将生产LLM服务引擎与通用PIM集成，通过分层粒度分配实现高效的关键值缓存管理，从而在注意力执行和运行时预填充重计算中获得平均1.95倍的速度提升和4.83倍的能源效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加速基于注意力机制的解码过程，尤其是在生产环境中，LLMs需要处理动态分配和管理逻辑键值缓存块（KV cache blocks）的需求。

Method: PATTON使用分层的粒度分配策略，K和V粒度块各自映射到逻辑令牌块，并固定其物理位置和指令生成；同时，更大的粒度用于高效执行GEMV和内存利用率。

Result: 在注意力执行和由运行时引发的预填充重计算中，PATTON实现了平均1.95倍的速度提升和4.83倍的能源效率提升。它无需PIM处理单元的修改就实现了这一目标。

Conclusion: PATTON的策略确保了在保持与本地GPU KV缓存相同的命中率的同时，能够在通用PIM上高效实现生产环境下的LLM服务。

Abstract: Processing-in-Memory (PIM) is promising for accelerating memory-bound decode attention, but attention acceleration alone is insufficient for production LLM serving, where engines dynamically allocate, populate, share, cache, and reclaim logical KV cache blocks. Supporting this lifecycle on commodity PIM requires efficient physical memory allocation, block-to-address mapping, and command generation. For the Value cache, these requirements create a fundamental conflict among GEMV efficiency, single-token write efficiency, and memory capacity: GEMV-optimized layouts scatter newly generated Value vectors across rows, making writes costly, while finer-grained memory sharing improves capacity utilization but fragments GEMV reductions. We present PATTON, a PIM runtime that integrates production LLM serving engines with commodity PIM. PATTON introduces hierarchical granule allocation: block-sized Key and Value granules map one-to-one to logical token blocks, fixing their physical placements and commands, while coarser granules group blocks for efficient GEMV execution and memory utilization. A Commit Zone stages partial Value blocks for efficient single-token writes before committing them to GEMV-optimized locations. PATTON tracks these placements to generate KV cache writes and QK-transpose/SV commands. Across attention execution and runtime-induced prefill recomputation, PATTON achieves an average 1.95x speedup and 4.83x higher energy efficiency over evaluated baselines, requires no PIM processing-unit modifications, and maintains a KV cache hit rate comparable to the native GPU KV cache in vLLM.

</details>


### [218] [CHERI-D Reincarnate: efficient multicore CHERI temporal memory safety through allocation reincarnation (draft version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11590)
*Yuecheng Wang,Jonathan Woodruff,Simon W. Moore*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: CHERI-D reincarnation（Reinc）通过动态重新分配ID而非释放内存来实现资源的连续复用，从而减少垃圾回收频率和隔离内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前作CHERI-D中的生成ID具有固定宽度且存储于固定位置，当该ID用尽时需要隔离对象。为了解决这个问题，Reinc提供了一种缓存ID的方式，允许在ID用尽时动态重新分配新的ID，同时保留了内存的即时重用能力。

Method: Reinc通过重新设计ID的处理方式，动态分配新ID来替代因ID用尽而产生的内存隔离机制。进一步地，引入了一致的ID缓存机制，并利用轻量级的反向映射和过滤机制支持多核执行。

Result: Reinc减少了垃圾回收的频率和隔离内存开销，同时维护了高地区性和低性能开销。

Conclusion: 该研究通过精细化管理内存ID，提供了更为高效的内存安全解决方案。

Abstract: We propose CHERI-D Reincarnate (Reinc), an architectural extension to CHERI for scalable and efficient temporal memory safety. Prior work CHERI-D has a finite-width generation ID stored at a fixed location, requiring an object to be quarantined when its ID is exhausted. Reinc further provides use-after-free mitigation while permitting immediate freed memory reuse for objects through allocation reincarnation: rather than quarantining an allocation slot upon ID exhaustion, Reinc dynamically assigns a new ID to that slot when its current ID is exhausted. Exhausted IDs are quarantined and later reclaimed, while the underlying memory remains available for immediate reuse. By quarantining IDs rather than memory, Reinc enables continuous reuse of memory in the common case, substantially reducing both memory-sweep frequency and quarantine memory overhead.
  Reinc further introduces coherent ID caching while retaining a fully decentralized ID organization. Temporal metadata remains colocated with the memory it protects, preserving locality while avoiding centralized metadata structures. To support multicore execution, Reinc connects physical coherence events to the virtually addressed ObjID buffer using lightweight reverse-map and filter-based mechanisms.
  We implement Reinc as a hardware-software co-design spanning CHERI-Toooba (superscalar FPGA softcore), QEMU, LLVM/Clang and CheriBSD. Across our evaluated workloads, Reinc substantially reduces memory-sweep frequency and memory quarantine while incurring low performance and hardware overhead.

</details>


### [219] [AccelForge: Comprehensive Modeling and Co-Design Framework for AI Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11906)
*Tanner Andrulis,Michael Gilbert,Vivienne Sze,Joel S. Emer*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: AccelForge 提供了一个具有强大功能、速度快且易于使用的加速器建模框架，它结合了可扩展的用户定义模型和快速映射工具，使研究人员能够快速评估和迭代加速器设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于现代数据中心和边缘部署中普遍存在能量密集型的张量代数任务（例如深度神经网络），需要加速器以实现能源效率和高吞吐量。加速器建模框架是快速评估和优化加速器设计的关键工具。

Method: AccelForge 通过整合多种工作，并提供了可组合的用户自定义和可修改的设备、电路和架构模型，以及快速的映射器来实现建模和优化。

Result: AccelForge 改进了现有加速器建模框架的功能、速度和易用性。它支持快速评估和优化，并且易于扩展以支持新的优化。

Conclusion: AccelForge 通过统一多种工具和优化方法，为加速器设计提供了高效的建模方式，加速了研究和开发过程。

Abstract: Tensor algebra workloads, of which deep neural networks are prominent examples, are energy-intensive workloads in modern datacenter and edge deployments, making accelerators necessary to achieve energy efficiency and high throughput. To quickly evaluate and iterate on accelerator designs, we need an accelerator modeling framework that captures salient attributes of devices, circuits, architectures, workloads, as well as optimizing the mapping of the workload onto the hardware.
  In this paper, we introduce AccelForge, which improves upon existing accelerator modeling frameworks in capabilities, speed, and ease-of-use. AccelForge unifies and multiple works into one framework, and it includes (1) composable user-defined and user-modifiable models of devices, circuits, and architectures, (2) fast mappers that enable accurate evaluation in orders of magnitude less (computer and human) time, and (3) easy-to-use and easy-to-extend, yet still high performance, Python implementations of both the model and mapper to enable rapid research and extension to novel optimizations.

</details>
